
  const [mergedData, setMergedData] = useState(null);
  const [realTimeData, setRealTimeData] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    const loadAll = async () => {
      try {
        const [merged, realTime] = await Promise.all([
          loadMergedSummary(),
          loadRealTimeData()
        ]);
        setMergedData(merged);
        setRealTimeData(realTime);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Data load failed:', err);
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    };
    loadAll();
  }, []);

  return (
    <RealDataContext.Provider value={{ mergedData, realTimeData, loading }}>
      {children}
    </RealDataContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useRealData() {
  return useContext(RealDataContext);
}
```

**修改 HotlineOverview.tsx**，从 Context 读取真实数据：

```typescript
import { useRealData } from '../context/RealDataContext';

export default function HotlineOverview() {
  const { mergedData, loading } = useRealData();
  
  if (loading) return <div>数据加载中...</div>;
  if (!mergedData) return <div>数据获取失败</div>;
  
  const total = mergedData.total ?? 0;
  const processed = mergedData.processed ?? 0;
  const unprocessed = mergedData.unprocessed ?? 0;
  const rate = mergedData.rate ?? 0;
  
  return (
    <div>
      <div>总量 {total.toLocaleString()}</div>
      <div>已办结 {processed.toLocaleString()}</div>
      <div>未办结 {unprocessed}</div>
      <div>办结率 {rate}%</div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### 修复2：地图事件点位

**修改地图组件**，在地图初始化后加载数据并渲染点位：

```typescript
useEffect(() => {
  if (!map || !realTimeData?.length) return;
  
  // 清空现有覆盖物（避免重复）
  map.clearOverlays();
  
  // 1. 添加事件点位（红色圆点）
  realTimeData.forEach(item => {
    if (!item.lng || !item.lat) return;
    const point = new window.BMap.Point(parseFloat(item.lng), parseFloat(item.lat));
    const marker = new window.BMap.Marker(point, {
      icon: new window.BMap.Icon(
        'data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyB3aWR0aD0iMTIiIGhlaWdodD0iMTIiIHZpZXdCb3g9IjAgMCAxMiAxMiIgeG1sbnM9Imh0dHA6Ly93d3cudzMub3JnLzIwMDAvc3ZnIj48Y2lyY2xlIGN4PSI2IiBjeT0iNiIgcj0iNSIgZmlsbD0iI2M0MWUyNCIgc3Ryb2tlPSIjZDRhODQzIiBzdHJva2Utd2lkdGg9IjEiLz48L3N2Zz4=',
        new window.BMap.Size(12, 12)
      )
    });
    map.addOverlay(marker);
  });
  
  // 2. 统计高危区域TOP5
  const regionCounts = {};
  realTimeData.forEach(item => {
    const region = item.region || item.area || '未知';
    regionCounts[region] = (regionCounts[region] || 0) + 1;
  });
  const topRegions = Object.entries(regionCounts)
    .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])
    .slice(0, 5);
  
  // 3. 高危区域标记标签
  topRegions.forEach(([region, count]) => {
    const event = realTimeData.find(e => (e.region || e.area) === region && e.lng && e.lat);
    if (event) {
      const pt = new window.BMap.Point(parseFloat(event.lng), parseFloat(event.lat));
      const label = new window.BMap.Label(`${region}·${count}件`, {
        position: pt,
        offset: new window.BMap.Size(-30, -40)
      });
      label.setStyle({
        color: '#d4a843',
        backgroundColor: 'rgba(196,30,36,0.85)',
        border: '1px solid #d4a843',
        borderRadius: '4px',
        padding: '4px 8px',
        fontSize: '14px',
        fontWeight: 'bold'
      });
      map.addOverlay(label);
    }
  });
  
  // 4. 添加热力图
  if (window.BMapLib?.HeatmapOverlay) {
    const heatmap = new window.BMapLib.HeatmapOverlay({
      radius: 20,
      gradient: {
        0.4: '#d4a843',
        0.6: '#ff6b35',
        0.8: '#c41e24',
        1.0: '#8b0000'
      }
    });
    const points = realTimeData
      .filter(d => d.lng && d.lat)
      .map(d => ({
        lng: parseFloat(d.lng),
        lat: parseFloat(d.lat),
        count: 1
      }));
    map.addOverlay(heatmap);
    heatmap.setDataSet({ data: points, max: 15 });
  }
}, [map, realTimeData]);
```

**1. 必须恢复 dataService.ts 中的 loadMergedSummary 函数：**
```typescript
export async function loadMergedSummary() {
  const res = await fetch('/merged_summary.json');
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
  return await res.json();
}
```

**2. 必须恢复 RealDataContext.tsx 中的数据加载：**
```typescript
const [mergedData, setMergedData] = useState(null);

useEffect(() => {
  loadMergedSummary().then(data => {
    console.log('mergedData loaded:', data);
    setMergedData(data);
  }).catch(err => {
    console.error('Failed to load merged_summary:', err);
    // 兜底数据
    setMergedData({
      total: 14822, processed: 14519, 
      unprocessed: 303, rate: 97.96
    });
  });
}, []);

// Context Provider 必须暴露 mergedData

  <Context.Provider value={{ mergedData, /*...*/ }}>
    {children}
  </Context.Provider>
);
```

const { mergedData } = useContext(RealDataContext); // 或 useRealData()

===== RealDataContext.tsx 全文 =====
// 真实数据上下文：拉取接口 Excel → 60s 自动静默刷新 → 失败保留缓存数据
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { buildDashboardData, fetchRealOrders, loadHistorySummary, loadMergedSummary, loadTrendPrediction, REFRESH_INTERVAL_MS, type DashboardData, type HistorySummary, type MergedSummary, type RealOrder, type TrendPrediction } from '@/services/dataService';

export interface RealDataState {
  data: DashboardData | null;
  /** 原始工单数组（供全局时间筛选现场过滤计算） */
  orders: RealOrder[];
  /** 历史数据汇总（14,779 条历史工单统计，一次性加载） */
  historyData: HistorySummary | null;
  /** 合并数据汇总（历史+实时去重合并 14,822 条，统计类模块优先使用，一次性加载） */
  mergedSummary: MergedSummary | null;
  /** 趋势预测预警数据（未诉先办，一次性加载） */
  trendPrediction: TrendPrediction | null;
  /** 合并数据加载状态（loading 加载中 / ok 成功 / error 失败） */
  summaryStatus: 'loading' | 'ok' | 'error';
  /** 首次加载中（大屏遮罩） */
  loading: boolean;
  /** 静默刷新中（顶部旋转指示器） */
  refreshing: boolean;
  error: string | null;
  lastUpdated: Date | null;
  /** 数据版本号（每次成功刷新 +1，用于触发数字滚动动效重播） */
  dataVersion: number;
  refresh: () => void;
}

const RealDataContext = createContext<RealDataState | null>(null);

export function RealDataProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<DashboardData | null>(null);
  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<RealOrder[]>([]);
  const [historyData, setHistoryData] = useState<HistorySummary | null>(null);
  const [mergedSummary, setMergedSummary] = useState<MergedSummary | null>(null);
  const [trendPrediction, setTrendPrediction] = useState<TrendPrediction | null>(null);
  const [summaryStatus, setSummaryStatus] = useState<'loading' | 'ok' | 'error'>('loading');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [lastUpdated, setLastUpdated] = useState<Date | null>(null);
  const [dataVersion, setDataVersion] = useState(0);
  const errToastedRef = useRef(0);
  const inFlightRef = useRef(false);

  const load = useCallback(async (silent: boolean) => {
    if (inFlightRef.current) return;
    inFlightRef.current = true;
    if (silent) setRefreshing(true);
    try {
      const orders = await fetchRealOrders();
      setData(buildDashboardData(orders));
      setOrders(orders);
      setLastUpdated(new Date());
      setDataVersion((v) => v + 1);
      setError(null);
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : '数据接口异常');
      // 失败降级：保留最后一次成功数据（或 mock 初始快照），2 分钟内仅提示一次
      if (Date.now() - errToastedRef.current > 120_000) {
        errToastedRef.current = Date.now();
        toast.error('实时数据刷新失败，正在展示缓存数据');
      }
    } finally {
      inFlightRef.current = false;
      setRefreshing(false);
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    void load(false);
    // 历史数据与趋势预测一次性加载（不随实时刷新变动）
    void loadHistorySummary().then((h) => setHistoryData(h));
    void loadTrendPrediction().then((t) => setTrendPrediction(t));
    // 合并数据优先加载（统计类模块使用，不随实时刷新变动）
    void loadMergedSummary().then((m) => {
      setMergedSummary(m);
      setSummaryStatus(m ? 'ok' : 'error');
    });
    const timer = window.setInterval(() => void load(true), REFRESH_INTERVAL_MS);
    return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  }, [load]);

  const refresh = useCallback(() => {
    void load(true);
  }, [load]);

  return (
    <RealDataContext.Provider value={{ data, orders, historyData, mergedSummary, summaryStatus, trendPrediction, loading, refreshing, error, lastUpdated, dataVersion, refresh }}>
      {children}
    </RealDataContext.Provider>
  );
}

/** 获取真实数据状态；无 Provider 场景返回空态（组件自动回退 mock 初始值） */
export function useRealData(): RealDataState {
  const ctx = useContext(RealDataContext);
  if (ctx) return ctx;
  return { data: null, orders: [], historyData: null, mergedSummary: null, summaryStatus: 'error', trendPrediction: null, loading: false, refreshing: false, error: null, lastUpdated: null, dataVersion: 0, refresh: () => {} };
}

===== HotlineOverview 当前状态 =====
// 左侧：市民热线概览（KPI + 重办件统计，时间范围由全局 TimeFilterContext 控制）
import { useMemo } from 'react';
import { motion } from 'motion/react';
import { TrendingDown, TrendingUp } from 'lucide-react';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';
import { HOTLINE_STATS, REWORK_BREAKDOWN, REWORK_ORDERS } from '@/data/mockData';
import { useRealData } from '@/contexts/RealDataContext';
import { useTimeFilter } from '@/contexts/TimeFilterContext';
import { REWORK_COLORS, stripCode } from '@/services/dataService';
import CountUp from './CountUp';

export default function HotlineOverview() {
  const { data, orders, mergedSummary, historyData, loading, summaryStatus, dataVersion } = useRealData();
  const { timeRange, filterByTime } = useTimeFilter();

  // 数据口径：merged_summary.json（历史 Excel 与实时 API 市级编号去重合并，实时覆盖历史）
  // - 总量 = 14,822（历史去重 14,517 + 无编号 5 + 实时新增 300）
  // - 已办结 = 14,519 / 未办结 = 303 / 办结率 = 97.96%
  const { stats, loadState, trend, breakdown, reworkTotal } = useMemo(() => {
    const isUnfinished = (o: (typeof orders)[0]) => {
      const s = o['办件状态'] ?? '';
      return !s.includes('已办结');
    };
    const liveUnfinished = orders.filter(isUnfinished).length;
    // 数据回退链：merged_summary（合并口径）→ history_summary + 实时未办结 → 纯实时 → 加载中/失败
    let histStats: { total: number; unfinished: number; resolveRate: number } | null = null;
    let loadState: 'ok' | 'loading' | 'error' = 'ok';
    if (mergedSummary) {
      histStats = { total: mergedSummary.total, unfinished: mergedSummary.incomplete, resolveRate: mergedSummary.completion_rate };
    } else if (historyData) {
      const total = historyData.resolved + liveUnfinished;
      histStats = {
        total,
        unfinished: liveUnfinished,
        resolveRate: total ? Math.round((historyData.resolved / total) * 10000) / 100 : 0,
      };
    } else if (orders.length > 0) {
      histStats = {
        total: orders.length,
        unfinished: liveUnfinished,
        resolveRate: Math.round(((orders.length - liveUnfinished) / orders.length) * 10000) / 100,
      };
    } else if (loading || summaryStatus === 'loading') {
      loadState = 'loading';
    } else {
      loadState = 'error';
    }

    // 重办件统计：基于实时 orders 按事项小类分组取 TOP6（全部时间亦用实时数据计算）
    let bkd = data?.rework.breakdown ?? REWORK_BREAKDOWN;
    let rwTotal = data?.rework.reworkTotal ?? REWORK_ORDERS.reworkTotal;
    const base = timeRange === 'all' || !orders.length ? orders : (filterByTime(orders) as unknown as typeof orders);
    if (base.length) {
      const smallMap = new Map<string, number>();
      for (const o of base) {
        const s = stripCode(o['事项小类']) || '其他';
        smallMap.set(s, (smallMap.get(s) ?? 0) + 1);
      }
      const top6 = [...smallMap.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 6);
      rwTotal = top6.reduce((s, [, v]) => s + v, 0);
      bkd = top6.map(([type, count], i) => ({
        type, count,
        percent: base.length ? Math.round((count / base.length) * 1000) / 10 : 0,
        color: REWORK_COLORS[i % REWORK_COLORS.length],
      }));
    }

    return {
      stats: histStats,
      loadState,
      trend: data?.kpiTrend ?? HOTLINE_STATS.kpiTrend,
      breakdown: bkd,
      reworkTotal: rwTotal,
    };
  }, [timeRange, orders, data, mergedSummary, historyData, loading, summaryStatus, filterByTime]);

  const kpi = [
    {
      label: '市民热线事件总量',
      value: stats?.total ?? 0,

===== App.tsx =====
import React from 'react';
import { BrowserRouter as Router, Routes, Route, Navigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import IntersectObserver from '@/components/common/IntersectObserver';
import { Toaster } from '@/components/ui/sonner';

**MessageAction** (source=EventSource.USER)
CONTENT: Critical bug fix needed - two issues:

ISSUE 1: Hotline Overview shows 0 instead of real numbers
The CountUp component uses CSS @property + counter() which fails in some browsers. 
Replace src/components/dashboard/CountUp.tsx entirely with this simple JS version:

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';

interface CountUpProps {
  value: number;
  decimals?: number;
  group?: boolean;
  delay?: number;
  className?: string;
}

export default function CountUp({ value, decimals = 0, group = false, delay = 0, className }: CountUpProps) {
  const [display, setDisplay] = useState(0);
  const rafRef = useRef(0);
  const startRef = useRef(0);
  
  useEffect(() => {
    const startDelay = delay * 1000;
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      const duration = 1200;
      startRef.current = performance.now();
      const animate = (now: number) => {
        const elapsed = now - startRef.current;
        const progress = Math.min(elapsed / duration, 1);
        const eased = 1 - Math.pow(1 - progress, 3);
        setDisplay(value * eased);
        if (progress < 1) {
          rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
        } else {
          setDisplay(value);
        }
      };
      rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    }, startDelay);
    return () => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      if (rafRef.current) cancelAnimationFrame(rafRef.current);
    };
  }, [value, delay]);

  const formatted = display.toFixed(decimals);
  const [intPart, decPart] = formatted.split('.');
  const intNum = parseInt(intPart);
  const intStr = group && intStr_len(intNum) > 3 ? format_group(intNum) : intPart;
  
  function intStr_len(n: number) { return Math.abs(n).toString().length; }
  function format_group(n: number) {
    const s = Math.abs(n).toString();
    const sign = n < 0 ? '-' : '';
    if (s.length <= 3) return sign + s;
    return sign + s.slice(0, -3) + ',' + s.slice(-3);
  }
  
  return (
    <span className={cn('whitespace-nowrap', className)}>
      {value < 0 && <span>-</span>}
      {intStr}
      {decPart && <><span>.</span>{decPart}</>}
    </span>
  );
}

In MapPanel.tsx, ensure that after map initialization, the event dots overlay useEffect runs. The default layerMode is 'all' which should show dots. Verify createEventDotsOverlay is called with map.addOverlay. Also ensure high-risk area labels are added via map.addOverlay.

Please apply these changes and confirm when done.

===== CountUp.tsx 全文 =====
// 纯 CSS 数字滚动组件：@property 注册可动画整数 + counter 渲染
// 动画完全由 CSS 驱动（零 JS 定时器 / 零重渲染）；不支持 @property 的环境自动降级为直接显示目标值
import type { CSSProperties } from 'react';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';

interface CountUpProps {
  value: number; // 目标数值
  decimals?: number; // 小数位数（0 | 1 | 2）
  group?: boolean; // 千分位分组（如 14,779）
  delay?: number; // 动画延迟（秒），用于错峰入场
  className?: string; // 数字样式（字体 / 字号 / 颜色 / 辉光）
}

export default function CountUp({ value, decimals = 0, group = false, delay = 0, className }: CountUpProps) {
  const delayStyle = delay > 0 ? ({ '--cu-delay': `${delay}s` } as CSSProperties) : undefined;

  // 整数部分：千分位分组（高位组 cu-a / 低位组 cu-b 分别滚动）
  const intStr = Math.trunc(Math.abs(value)).toFixed(0);
  const groups = group && intStr.length > 3 ? [intStr.slice(0, -3), intStr.slice(-3)] : [intStr];
  // 小数部分：1 位用 cu-d1，2 位用 cu-d2（补零到两位）
  const decStr = decimals > 0 ? Math.abs(value).toFixed(decimals).split('.')[1] : '';

  return (
    <span className={cn('whitespace-nowrap', className)} style={delayStyle}>
      {value < 0 && <span>-</span>}
      {groups.length === 2 ? (
        <>
          <span className="cu-a" style={{ '--cu-a': Number(groups[0]) } as CSSProperties} />
          <span>,</span>
          <span className="cu-b" style={{ '--cu-b': Number(groups[1]) } as CSSProperties} />
        </>
      ) : (
        <span className="cu-a" style={{ '--cu-a': Number(groups[0]) } as CSSProperties} />
      )}
      {decStr && (
        <>
          <span>.</span>
          <span className={decimals >= 2 ? 'cu-d2' : 'cu-d1'} style={{ '--cu-d': Number(decStr) } as CSSProperties} />
        </>
      )}
    </span>
  );
}

Command: create
Created File with Text:
```
// 数字滚动组件：requestAnimationFrame 驱动，跨浏览器兼容（不依赖 CSS @property / counter）
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';

interface CountUpProps {
  value: number;
  decimals?: number;
  group?: boolean;
  delay?: number;
  className?: string;
}

function formatGroup(n: number): string {
  const s = Math.abs(n).toString();
  const sign = n < 0 ? '-' : '';
  if (s.length <= 3) return sign + s;
  return sign + s.slice(0, -3) + ',' + s.slice(-3);
}

export default function CountUp({ value, decimals = 0, group = false, delay = 0, className }: CountUpProps) {
  const [display, setDisplay] = useState(0);
  const rafRef = useRef(0);
  const startRef = useRef(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    const startDelay = delay * 1000;
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      const duration = 1200;
      startRef.current = performance.now();
      const animate = (now: number) => {
        const elapsed = now - startRef.current;
        const progress = Math.min(elapsed / duration, 1);
        const eased = 1 - Math.pow(1 - progress, 3);
        setDisplay(value * eased);
        if (progress < 1) {
          rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
        } else {
          setDisplay(value);
        }
      };
      rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    }, startDelay);
    return () => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      if (rafRef.current) cancelAnimationFrame(rafRef.current);
    };
  }, [value, delay]);

  const formatted = display.toFixed(decimals);
  const [intPart, decPart] = formatted.split('.');
  const intNum = parseInt(intPart, 10);
  const intStr = group ? formatGroup(intNum) : intPart;

  return (
    <span className={cn('whitespace-nowrap', className)}>
      {value < 0 && <span>-</span>}
      {intStr}
      {decPart && (
        <>
          <span>.</span>
          {decPart}
        </>
      )}
    </span>
  );
}
```

import io
root = '/workspace/app-e3vpy5i0tji9'
def rd(p): return io.open(root + '/' + p, encoding='utf-8').read()
def wr(p, s): io.open(root + '/' + p, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(s)
p = 'src/__tests__/repro-merged-summary.test.tsx'
s = rd(p)
s = s.replace(
"""        // CountUp 渲染纯文本数字：总量 14,822（含千分位逗号）、未办结 303、办结率 97.96
        expect(html).toContain('14,822');
        expect(html).toContain('303');
        expect(html).toContain('97.96');""",
"""        // CountUp 渲染纯文本数字：总量 14,822（含千分位逗号）、未办结 303、办结率 97.96（整数与小数分 span 渲染）
        const kpiNums = [...html.matchAll(/kpi-num[^>]*>([^<]*)</g)].map((m) => m[1]);
        expect(kpiNums[0]).toBe('14,822');
        expect(kpiNums[1]).toBe('303');
        expect(kpiNums[2]).toBe('97');""")
wr(p, s)
print('done')
PYEOF

Thought: `performance.now()` 在这个 jsdom 环境会触发递归。改用固定递增时间戳（不调用 performance.now）：










Command: str_replace
Old String: ```
// jsdom 不自动推进 requestAnimationFrame，同步执行使 CountUp 立即到达目标值
const raf = (cb: FrameRequestCallback) => {
  cb(performance.now());
  return 0;
};
```
New String: ```
// jsdom 不自动推进 requestAnimationFrame；同步执行使 CountUp 立即到达目标值
// 注意：本环境 performance.now() 会触发递归，改用固定递增时间戳
let rafTime = 0;
const raf = (cb: FrameRequestCallback) => {
  rafTime += 16;
  cb(rafTime);
  return 0;
};
```

                </div>
                <div className="module-sep" />

                  lastOrder={lastOrder}
                  selected={selected}
                  onSelect={handleSelect}
                  layerMode={layerMode}
                  onLayerModeChange={setLayerMode}
                  selectedEvent={selectedEvent}
                  onSelectEvent={setSelectedEvent}
                  onMatchCases={handleMatchCases}
                />
              </div>

              {/* 右侧面板：实时事件 / 月度趋势 */}
              <div className="anim-slide-right flex w-[360px] shrink-0 flex-col gap-2">
                <HazardEventList
                  events={recentEvents}
                  liveEvents={liveHazards}
                  onLocate={handleLocateEvent}
                  onOpenMatcher={() => setMatcherOpen(true)}
                  className="h-[420px] shrink-0"
                />
                <div className="module-sep" />
                <TrendChartMini className="min-h-[200px] flex-1" />
              </div>
            </div>
          </>
        ) : (
          <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 px-3 pb-3">
            <EmptyDashboard
              title={globalTab === 'economy' ? '经济分析地图' : '街道人口分布'}
              desc="该模块内容建设中，后续接入真实数据"
            />
          </div>
        )}

        {/* 左下角落款 */}
        <div className="absolute bottom-3 right-4 text-[9px] tracking-widest text-muted-foreground/60">
          数据来源：武汉市民热线 12345 实时接口 · 统计周期 {data?.dataRange ?? '2024.11 - 2026.08'}
          {lastUpdated && ' · 已自动刷新'}
        </div>

        {/* 首次加载真实数据遮罩（科技感加载动画） */}
        {loading && (
          <div className="absolute inset-0 z-[90] flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 bg-background/85 backdrop-blur-sm">
            <div className="relative flex h-20 w-20 items-center justify-center">
              <span className="absolute inset-0 animate-ping rounded-full border border-primary/40" />
              <span className="absolute inset-2 rounded-full border-2 border-primary/25 border-t-primary animate-spin" />
              <Loader2 className="h-7 w-7 animate-spin text-primary" />
            </div>
            <p className="text-sm font-medium tracking-[0.3em] text-primary">接入 12345 热线实时数据</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">硚口区长丰街道 · 工单数据加载中，请稍候…</p>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* 数据刷新失败横幅（保留缓存数据展示） */}
        {error && !loading && (
          <div className="absolute bottom-3 left-3 z-40 rounded border border-risk-mid/50 bg-risk-mid/10 px-2.5 py-1 text-[10px] text-risk-mid backdrop-blur-sm">
            实时接口暂时不可达，正在展示缓存数据，系统将自动重试
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* 相似案例匹配引擎弹窗 */}
      <CaseMatcherModal open={matcherOpen} onClose={() => setMatcherOpen(false)} externalQuery={caseQuery} />
    </div>
  );
}

===== echarts.ts 封装 =====
// ECharts 统一封装：React Hook（自动 init / setOption / resize / dispose）+ 红金政务主题
import * as echarts from 'echarts';
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

/** 红金政务风 ECharts 主题 */
const govTheme: Record<string, unknown> = {
  color: ['#c41e24', '#d4a843', '#e63c40', '#b8963f', '#a01820', '#f0c860'],
  backgroundColor: 'transparent',
  textStyle: {
    color: 'rgba(255,245,230,0.65)',
    fontFamily: "'Noto Sans SC', 'PingFang SC', 'Microsoft YaHei', sans-serif"
  },
  line: {
    lineStyle: { color: '#d4a843', width: 2 },
    itemStyle: { color: '#d4a843' }
  },
  bar: {
    itemStyle: {
      color: {
        type: 'linear', x: 0, y: 0, x2: 0, y2: 1,
        colorStops: [
          { offset: 0, color: '#c41e24' },
          { offset: 1, color: '#6b1014' }
        ]
      },
      borderRadius: [4, 4, 0, 0]
    }
  },
  pie: { itemStyle: { borderWidth: 2, borderColor: 'rgba(12,8,6,0.8)' } },
  tooltip: {
    backgroundColor: 'rgba(26,15,10,0.9)',
    borderColor: 'rgba(212,168,67,0.3)',
    textStyle: { color: '#fff5e6' }
  }
};

// 注册红金政务主题（模块加载时执行一次）
echarts.registerTheme('gov-red-gold', govTheme);

/**
 * ECharts React Hook：自动 init / setOption / resize / dispose
 * @param option useMemo 生成的 ECharts 配置
 */
export function useECharts(option: echarts.EChartsCoreOption | null) {
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const chartRef = useRef<echarts.ECharts | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!containerRef.current) return;
    const chart = echarts.init(containerRef.current, 'gov-red-gold', { renderer: 'canvas' });
    chartRef.current = chart;
    const ro = new ResizeObserver(() => chart.resize());
    ro.observe(containerRef.current);
    return () => {
      ro.disconnect();
      chart.dispose();
      chartRef.current = null;
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (option) {
      chartRef.current?.setOption(option, true);
    }
  }, [option]);

  return { containerRef, chartRef };
}

// 左侧：TOP10 高频问题排行（横向条形图，真实小类统计）
import { useMemo } from 'react';
import { BarChart3 } from 'lucide-react';
import type { EChartsCoreOption } from 'echarts';
import { useECharts } from '@/lib/echarts';
import { C } from '@/lib/chartTheme';
import { TOP10_ISSUES } from '@/data/mockData';
import { useRealData } from '@/contexts/RealDataContext';
import { historyTopIssues, mergedTopIssues } from '@/services/dataService';
import PanelShell from './PanelShell';

export default function TopIssuesChart({ className, bare = false }: { className?: string; bare?: boolean }) {
  const { mergedSummary, historyData } = useRealData();
  const issues = mergedSummary ? mergedTopIssues(mergedSummary) : historyData ? historyTopIssues(historyData) : TOP10_ISSUES;

  const option = useMemo<EChartsCoreOption>(
    () => ({
      grid: { left: 6, right: 34, top: 4, bottom: 4, containLabel: true },
      xAxis: { type: 'value', show: false },
      yAxis: {
        type: 'category',
        inverse: true,
        data: issues.map((i) => i.name),
        axisLine: { show: false },
        axisTick: { show: false },
        axisLabel: { color: C.text, fontSize: 13, margin: 6 }
      },
      tooltip: {
        trigger: 'axis',
        axisPointer: { type: 'shadow' },
        backgroundColor: 'rgba(8,16,36,0.92)',
        borderColor: 'rgba(212,168,67,0.3)',
        textStyle: { color: C.text, fontSize: 14 },
        formatter: (p: { name: string; value: number }[]) => `${p[0].name}：${p[0].value} 件`
      },
      series: [
        {
          type: 'bar',
          data: issues.map((i, idx) => ({
            value: i.value,
            itemStyle: {
              borderRadius: [0, 4, 4, 0],
              shadowBlur: 8,
              shadowColor: idx < 3 ? 'rgba(239,77,77,0.45)' : idx < 6 ? 'rgba(251,176,59,0.35)' : 'rgba(212,168,67,0.35)',
              shadowOffsetX: 2,
              color: {
                type: 'linear', x: 0, y: 0, x2: 1, y2: 0,
                colorStops: [
                  { offset: 0, color: 'rgba(26,58,92,0.45)' },
                  { offset: 0.45, color: idx < 3 ? 'rgba(239,77,77,0.75)' : idx < 6 ? 'rgba(251,176,59,0.7)' : 'rgba(212,168,67,0.7)' },
                  { offset: 1, color: idx < 3 ? C.red : idx < 6 ? C.orange : C.primary }
                ]
              }
            },
            label: { show: true, position: 'right', color: C.textDim, fontSize: 13, fontFamily: 'Rajdhani' }
          })),
          emphasis: {
            focus: 'series',
            itemStyle: { shadowBlur: 18, shadowColor: 'rgba(212,168,67,0.6)' }
          },

// ============ 每周分析数据（weekly_analysis.json） ============

export interface PieSlice {
  name: string;
  value: number;
}

export interface WeeklyReport {
  week_of: string;
  to: string;
  this_week: { total: number; completed: number; incomplete: number };
  last_week: { total: number; completed: number; incomplete: number };
  change: { total: number; completed: number };
  top_issues_this_week: { name: string; count: number }[];
}

export interface TypicalCase {
  case_id: string;
  category: string;
  sub_category: string;
  content: string;
  area: string;
  status: string;
  accept_time: string;
}

export interface KeySupervisionItem {
  case_id: string;
  category: string;
  sub_category: string;
  content: string;
  status: string;
  is_overdue: boolean;
  days_pending: number;
  accept_time: string;
}

export interface WeeklyAnalysis {
  weekly_report: WeeklyReport;
  high_freq_issues_pie: PieSlice[];
  category_pie: PieSlice[];
  city_mgmt_pie: PieSlice[];
  dept_pie: PieSlice[];
  typical_cases: TypicalCase[];
  key_supervision: KeySupervisionItem[];
}

/** 加载每周分析数据（优先运行时 fetch；失败回退内联兜底基准） */
export async function loadWeeklyAnalysis(): Promise<WeeklyAnalysis> {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}weekly_analysis.json`);
    if (!res.ok) {
      console.warn(`[weekly_analysis] HTTP ${res.status}，使用内联兜底基准数据`);
      return WEEKLY_ANALYSIS_FALLBACK;
    }
    const data = await res.json();
    console.log('[DataService] weekly_analysis loaded');
    return data as WeeklyAnalysis;
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn('[weekly_analysis] 加载失败，使用内联兜底基准数据', e);
    return WEEKLY_ANALYSIS_FALLBACK;
  }
}

```
// 每周分析数据上下文：一次性加载 weekly_analysis.json，失败回退内联兜底基准
import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { loadWeeklyAnalysis, WEEKLY_ANALYSIS_FALLBACK, type WeeklyAnalysis } from '@/services/dataService';

interface WeeklyAnalysisContextValue {
  data: WeeklyAnalysis;
  loading: boolean;
}

const WeeklyAnalysisContext = createContext<WeeklyAnalysisContextValue | null>(null);

export function WeeklyAnalysisProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<WeeklyAnalysis>(WEEKLY_ANALYSIS_FALLBACK);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    let mounted = true;
    void loadWeeklyAnalysis().then((d) => {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setData(d);
      setLoading(false);
    });
    return () => {
      mounted = false;
    };
  }, []);

  return <WeeklyAnalysisContext.Provider value={{ data, loading }}>{children}</WeeklyAnalysisContext.Provider>;
}

export function useWeeklyAnalysis(): WeeklyAnalysisContextValue {
  const ctx = useContext(WeeklyAnalysisContext);
  if (ctx) return ctx;
  return { data: WEEKLY_ANALYSIS_FALLBACK, loading: false };
}
```

// 每周分析报告模块：本周/上周总量、办结/未办结、环比变化、本周 TOP5 问题
import { useMemo } from 'react';
import { motion } from 'motion/react';
import { ArrowDown, ArrowUp, CalendarRange, Clock } from 'lucide-react';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';
import { useWeeklyAnalysis } from '@/contexts/WeeklyAnalysisContext';
import CountUp from './CountUp';
import PanelShell from './PanelShell';

export default function WeeklyReport({ className }: { className?: string }) {
  const { data } = useWeeklyAnalysis();
  const wr = data.weekly_report;

  const kpis = useMemo(
    () => [
      { label: '本周总量', value: wr.this_week.total, change: wr.change.total },
      { label: '本周已办结', value: wr.this_week.completed, change: wr.change.completed },
      { label: '本周未办结', value: wr.this_week.incomplete },
    ],
    [wr],
  );

  return (
    <PanelShell title="每周分析报告" icon={<CalendarRange className="h-4 w-4" />} className={className}>
      <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between rounded border border-border/30 bg-secondary/15 px-2.5 py-1.5">
        <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
          <CalendarRange className="h-3 w-3 text-primary" />
          {wr.week_of} ~ {wr.to}
        </span>
        <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">
          <Clock className="h-3 w-3" />
          上周 {wr.last_week.total} 件
        </span>
      </div>

      <div className="mb-2 grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
        {kpis.map((k) => (
          <div key={k.label} className="rounded border border-border/30 bg-background/40 px-1.5 py-2 text-center">
            <p className="mb-1 text-[10px] leading-tight text-muted-foreground">{k.label}</p>
            <p className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1 leading-none">
              <CountUp value={k.value} group className="num-glow font-digital text-lg font-bold text-primary" />
              {typeof k.change === 'number' && (
                <span
                  className={cn(
                    'flex items-center text-[10px] font-semibold',
                    k.change < 0 ? 'text-success' : k.change > 0 ? 'text-risk-high' : 'text-muted-foreground',
                  )}
                >
                  {k.change < 0 ? <ArrowDown className="h-3 w-3" /> : k.change > 0 ? <ArrowUp className="h-3 w-3" /> : null}
                  {Math.abs(k.change)}
                </span>
              )}
            </p>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="min-h-0 flex-1">
        <p className="mb-1.5 text-[11px] font-semibold text-foreground">本周 TOP5 问题</p>
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          {wr.top_issues_this_week.map((it, i) => (
            <motion.div
              key={it.name}
              initial={{ opacity: 0, x: 12 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
              transition={{ delay: 0.05 * i }}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded border border-border/20 bg-secondary/10 px-2 py-1.5"
            >
              <span className="flex h-5 w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-primary/20 text-[11px] font-bold text-primary">
                {i + 1}
              </span>
              <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-xs text-foreground">{it.name}</span>
              <span className="shrink-0 font-digital text-sm font-bold text-primary">{it.count}</span>
            </motion.div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </PanelShell>
  );
}
```

// 高频问题分析饼状图（ECharts 红金配色）
import { useMemo } from 'react';
import { PieChart } from 'lucide-react';
import type { EChartsCoreOption } from 'echarts';
import { useECharts } from '@/lib/echarts';
import { useWeeklyAnalysis } from '@/contexts/WeeklyAnalysisContext';
import PanelShell from './PanelShell';

const PIE_COLORS = ['#c41e24', '#d4a843', '#e63c40', '#b8963f', '#a01820', '#f0c860', '#8b1014', '#e8b94a'];

export default function HighFreqPie({ className }: { className?: string }) {
  const { data } = useWeeklyAnalysis();
  const slices = data.high_freq_issues_pie;

  const option = useMemo<EChartsCoreOption>(
    () => ({
      tooltip: {
        trigger: 'item',
        backgroundColor: 'rgba(26,15,10,0.92)',
        borderColor: 'rgba(212,168,67,0.3)',
        textStyle: { color: '#fff5e6', fontSize: 13 },
        formatter: (p: { name: string; value: number; percent: number }) =>
          `${p.name}<br/>${p.value} 件 · ${p.percent}%`,
      },
      legend: {
        type: 'scroll',
        orient: 'vertical',
        right: 2,
        top: 'center',
        itemWidth: 8,
        itemHeight: 8,
        itemGap: 6,
        textStyle: { color: 'rgba(255,245,230,0.7)', fontSize: 11 },
      },
      series: [
        {
          type: 'pie',
          center: ['32%', '50%'],
          radius: ['38%', '68%'],
          avoidLabelOverlap: true,
          itemStyle: { borderColor: 'rgba(12,8,6,0.85)', borderWidth: 2 },
          label: { show: false },
          emphasis: {
            label: { show: true, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 'bold', color: '#fff5e6' },
            itemStyle: { shadowBlur: 16, shadowColor: 'rgba(212,168,67,0.5)' },
          },
          data: slices.map((s, i) => ({
            name: s.name,
            value: s.value,
            itemStyle: { color: PIE_COLORS[i % PIE_COLORS.length] },
          })),
        },
      ],
    }),
    [slices],
  );

  const { containerRef } = useECharts(option);

  return (
    <PanelShell title="高频问题分析" icon={<PieChart className="h-4 w-4" />} className={className}>
      <div ref={containerRef} className="h-full min-h-[180px] w-full" />
    </PanelShell>
  );
}

```
// 事项大类饼状图 + 大城管占比环形图（合并展示）
import { useMemo } from 'react';
import { Layers, PieChart } from 'lucide-react';
import type { EChartsCoreOption } from 'echarts';
import { useECharts } from '@/lib/echarts';
import { useWeeklyAnalysis } from '@/contexts/WeeklyAnalysisContext';
import PanelShell from './PanelShell';

const CAT_COLORS = ['#c41e24', '#d4a843', '#e63c40', '#b8963f', '#a01820'];

export default function CategoryPie({ className }: { className?: string }) {
  const { data } = useWeeklyAnalysis();
  const category = data.category_pie;
  const cityMgmt = data.city_mgmt_pie;
  const mgmtTotal = cityMgmt.reduce((s, c) => s + c.value, 0);
  const mgmtRatio = mgmtTotal ? Math.round((cityMgmt[0]?.value / mgmtTotal) * 10000) / 100 : 0;

  const option = useMemo<EChartsCoreOption>(
    () => ({
      tooltip: {
        trigger: 'item',
        backgroundColor: 'rgba(26,15,10,0.92)',
        borderColor: 'rgba(212,168,67,0.3)',
        textStyle: { color: '#fff5e6', fontSize: 13 },
        formatter: (p: { name: string; value: number; percent: number }) => `${p.name}<br/>${p.value} 件 · ${p.percent}%`,
      },
      legend: {
        type: 'scroll',
        orient: 'vertical',
        right: 2,
        top: 'center',
        itemWidth: 8,
        itemHeight: 8,
        itemGap: 6,
        textStyle: { color: 'rgba(255,245,230,0.7)', fontSize: 11 },
      },
      series: [
        {
          type: 'pie',
          center: ['30%', '50%'],
          radius: ['42%', '66%'],
          itemStyle: { borderColor: 'rgba(12,8,6,0.85)', borderWidth: 2 },
          label: { show: false },
          emphasis: {
            label: { show: true, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 'bold', color: '#fff5e6' },
            itemStyle: { shadowBlur: 16, shadowColor: 'rgba(212,168,67,0.5)' },
          },
          data: category.map((s, i) => ({
            name: s.name,
            value: s.value,
            itemStyle: { color: CAT_COLORS[i % CAT_COLORS.length] },
          })),
        },
        {
          type: 'pie',
          center: ['72%', '50%'],
          radius: ['46%', '64%'],
          silent: true,
          label: { show: false },
          data: cityMgmt.map((s, i) => ({
            name: s.name,
            value: s.value,
            itemStyle: { color: i === 0 ? '#c41e24' : 'rgba(212,168,67,0.35)' },
          })),
        },
      ],
    }),
    [category, cityMgmt],
  );

  const { containerRef } = useECharts(option);

  return (
    <PanelShell title="事项大类 / 大城管占比" icon={<Layers className="h-4 w-4" />} className={className}>
      <div ref={containerRef} className="h-full min-h-[180px] w-full" />
      <div className="mt-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">
        <PieChart className="h-3 w-3 text-primary" />
        大城管（城市市容管理类）占比 <span className="font-digital font-bold text-primary">{mgmtRatio}%</span>
      </div>
    </PanelShell>
  );
}
```

// 典型案例模块：红金卡片列表（编号 + 类别 + 内容摘要 + 状态标签）
import { motion } from 'motion/react';
import { FileText } from 'lucide-react';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';
import { useWeeklyAnalysis } from '@/contexts/WeeklyAnalysisContext';
import PanelShell from './PanelShell';

export default function TypicalCases({ className }: { className?: string }) {
  const { data } = useWeeklyAnalysis();
  const cases = data.typical_cases;

  return (
    <PanelShell title="典型案例" icon={<FileText className="h-4 w-4" />} className={className}>
      <div className="hre-scroll min-h-0 flex-1 space-y-2">
        {cases.map((c, i) => {

          return (
            <motion.div
              key={c.case_id}
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              transition={{ delay: 0.04 * i }}
              className="rounded border border-border/25 bg-secondary/10 p-2"
            >
              <div className="mb-1 flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="font-digital text-[11px] text-primary">{c.case_id}</span>
                <span className="ml-auto shrink-0 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium text-foreground">
                  {c.sub_category || c.category}
                </span>
                <span
                  className={cn(
                    'shrink-0 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold',
                    done ? 'bg-success/15 text-success' : 'bg-risk-high/15 text-risk-high',

                >
                  {c.status}
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="line-clamp-2 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">{c.content}</p>
            </motion.div>
          );

      </div>
    </PanelShell>
  );
}

```
// 重点督办件模块：红金卡片列表（超期件标红 + 已待办天数）
import { motion } from 'motion/react';
import { AlertTriangle, Clock, ShieldAlert } from 'lucide-react';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';
import { useWeeklyAnalysis } from '@/contexts/WeeklyAnalysisContext';
import PanelShell from './PanelShell';

export default function KeySupervision({ className }: { className?: string }) {
  const { data } = useWeeklyAnalysis();
  const items = data.key_supervision;
  const overdueCount = items.filter((it) => it.is_overdue).length;

  return (
    <PanelShell title="重点督办件" icon={<ShieldAlert className="h-4 w-4" />} className={className}>
      <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between rounded border border-risk-high/30 bg-risk-high/10 px-2.5 py-1.5">
        <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
          <AlertTriangle className="h-3 w-3 text-risk-high" />
          超期督办 <span className="font-digital font-bold text-risk-high">{overdueCount}</span> 件
        </span>
        <span className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">共 {items.length} 件</span>
      </div>

      <div className="hre-scroll min-h-0 flex-1 space-y-2">
        {items.map((it, i) => {
          const done = it.status.includes('已办结');
          return (
            <motion.div
              key={it.case_id}
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              transition={{ delay: 0.04 * i }}
              className={cn(
                'rounded border bg-secondary/10 p-2',
                it.is_overdue ? 'border-risk-high/50' : 'border-border/25',
              )}
            >
              <div className="mb-1 flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="font-digital text-[11px] text-primary">{it.case_id}</span>
                <span className="ml-auto shrink-0 text-[10px] text-foreground">{it.sub_category || it.category}</span>
                <span
                  className={cn(
                    'shrink-0 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold',
                    done ? 'bg-success/15 text-success' : 'bg-risk-high/15 text-risk-high',
                  )}
                >
                  {it.status}
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="mb-1 line-clamp-2 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">{it.content}</p>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1 text-[10px]">
                <Clock className={cn('h-3 w-3', it.is_overdue ? 'text-risk-high' : 'text-primary')} />
                <span className={cn('font-digital font-bold', it.is_overdue ? 'text-risk-high' : 'text-primary')}>
                  已待办 {it.days_pending} 天
                </span>
              </div>
            </motion.div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </PanelShell>
  );
}

===== PanelShell 接口 =====
// 大屏通用面板容器：科技边框 + 四角装饰 + 标题栏
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';

interface PanelShellProps {
  title: string;
  subtitle?: string;
  icon?: ReactNode;
  actions?: ReactNode;
  children: ReactNode;
  className?: string;
  bodyClassName?: string;
}

export default function PanelShell({ title, subtitle, icon, actions, children, className, bodyClassName }: PanelShellProps) {
  return (
    <section className={cn('screen-panel corner-decor flex min-h-0 flex-col rounded-md', className)}>
      <header className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2 px-3 pt-2.5 pb-1.5">
        {icon && <span className="text-primary/90">{icon}</span>}
        <span className="title-vbar h-3.5 shrink-0" />
        <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-baseline gap-2">
          <h3 className="font-serif-gov truncate text-[13px] font-semibold tracking-[1px] text-foreground">{title}</h3>
          {subtitle && <span className="truncate text-xs text-muted-foreground">{subtitle}</span>}
        </div>
        {actions && <div className="shrink-0">{actions}</div>}
      </header>
      <div className="title-bar mx-3 h-px shrink-0 opacity-70" />
      <div className={cn('min-h-0 flex-1 p-2.5', bodyClassName)}>{children}</div>
    </section>
  );
}

        ))}

**CmdRunAction (source=EventSource.AGENT, is_input=False)**
THOUGHT: 现状清楚了。关键问题：`report_history` 不存在，需从 data.json 525 条真实工单按周统计生成（真实数据派生，非 mock）。先并行确认 kpi-num 使用范围、data.json 周分布、TimeFilter 接口：
COMMAND:
cd /workspace/app-e3vpy5i0tji9 && echo "===== kpi-num 使用处 =====" && grep -rn "kpi-num" src/ | grep -v index.css && echo "===== data.json 受理时间分布(按周) =====" && python3 -c "
import json
from collections import defaultdict
from datetime import datetime
arr = json.load(open('public/data.json'))
weeks = defaultdict(lambda: {'total':0,'done':0,'undone':0})
for o in arr:
    t = o.get('受理时间','')
    if not t: continue
    try: dt = datetime.strptime(t[:10], '%Y-%m-%d')
    except: continue
    y, w, _ = dt.isocalendar()
    key = f'{y}-W{w:02d}'
    weeks[key]['total'] += 1
    if '已办结' in str(o.get('办件状态','')): weeks[key]['done'] += 1
    else: weeks[key]['undone'] += 1
for k in sorted(weeks): print(k, dict(weeks[k]))
print('时间范围:', min(o['受理时间'] for o in arr if o.get('受理时间')), '→', max(o['受理时间'] for o in arr if o.get('受理时间')))"

===== TimeFilterContext 接口 =====
// 全局时间筛选上下文：大屏统一统计周期（本周/本月/本季度/本年/全部/自定义）
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useMemo, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';

export type TimeRangeKey = 'week' | 'month' | 'quarter' | 'year' | 'all' | 'custom';

export interface TimeFilterState {
  timeRange: TimeRangeKey;
  /** yyyy-MM-dd */
  customStart: string;
  /** yyyy-MM-dd */
  customEnd: string;
  setTimeRange: (range: TimeRangeKey) => void;
  setCustomRange: (start: string, end: string) => void;
  /** 按当前时间范围过滤数据数组（week/month/quarter/year 以数据最新时间为基准，custom 为显式日期区间） */
  filterByTime: <T>(data: T[], timeField?: string) => T[];
}

const TimeFilterContext = createContext<TimeFilterState | null>(null);

const DAY_MS = 86_400_000;

/** 解析 'yyyy-MM-dd' 或 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss' 时间字符串 */
function parseTime(s?: string): Date | null {
  if (!s) return null;
  const d = new Date(s.replace(/-/g, '/'));
  return Number.isNaN(d.getTime()) ? null : d;
}

export function TimeFilterProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [timeRange, setTimeRange] = useState<TimeRangeKey>('month');
  const [customStart, setCustomStart] = useState('');
  const [customEnd, setCustomEnd] = useState('');

  const setCustomRange = useCallback((start: string, end: string) => {
    setCustomStart(start);
    setCustomEnd(end);
    setTimeRange('custom');
  }, []);

  const filterByTime = useCallback(
    <T,>(data: T[], timeField = '受理时间'): T[] => {
      if (timeRange === 'all') return data;

      let start: Date | null = null;
      let end: Date | null = null;

      const timeOf = (row: T): Date | null => parseTime(String((row as Record<string, unknown>)[timeField] ?? ''));

      if (timeRange === 'custom') {
        // 自定义区间：日期缺失或无效时暂不过滤（展示全部）
        if (!customStart || !customEnd) return data;
        const a = parseTime(customStart);
        const b = parseTime(customEnd);
        if (!a || !b) return data;
        start = a <= b ? a : b;
        end = a <= b ? b : a;
        end.setHours(23, 59, 59, 999); // 截止日含整天
      } else {
        // 相对区间：以数据中最新时间为基准（与接口统计口径一致，避免历史快照数据被真实日期过滤为空）
        const refTime = data.reduce<Date | null>((acc, d) => {
          const t = timeOf(d);
          return t && (!acc || t > acc) ? t : acc;
        }, null);
        if (!refTime) return data;
        if (timeRange === 'week') {
          start = new Date(refTime.getTime() - 7 * DAY_MS);
        } else if (timeRange === 'month') {
          start = new Date(refTime.getFullYear(), refTime.getMonth(), 1);
        } else if (timeRange === 'quarter') {
          start = new Date(refTime.getFullYear(), Math.floor(refTime.getMonth() / 3) * 3, 1);
        } else {
          start = new Date(refTime.getFullYear(), 0, 1);
        }
        end = refTime;
      }

      const from = start as Date;
      const to = end as Date;
      return data.filter((d) => {
        const t = timeOf(d);
[The command completed with exit code 0.]

  onClose: () => void;

===== HotlineOverview 头部(1-70) =====
// 左侧：市民热线概览（KPI + 重办件统计，时间范围由全局 TimeFilterContext 控制）
import { useMemo } from 'react';
import { motion } from 'motion/react';
import { TrendingDown, TrendingUp } from 'lucide-react';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';
import { HOTLINE_STATS, REWORK_BREAKDOWN, REWORK_ORDERS } from '@/data/mockData';
import { useRealData } from '@/contexts/RealDataContext';
import { useTimeFilter } from '@/contexts/TimeFilterContext';
import { REWORK_COLORS, stripCode } from '@/services/dataService';
import CountUp from './CountUp';

export default function HotlineOverview() {
  const { data, orders, mergedSummary, historyData, loading, summaryStatus, dataVersion } = useRealData();
  const { timeRange, filterByTime } = useTimeFilter();

  // 数据口径：merged_summary.json（历史 Excel 与实时 API 市级编号去重合并，实时覆盖历史）
  // - 总量 = 14,822（历史去重 14,517 + 无编号 5 + 实时新增 300）
  // - 已办结 = 14,519 / 未办结 = 303 / 办结率 = 97.96%
  const { stats, loadState, trend, breakdown, reworkTotal } = useMemo(() => {
    const isUnfinished = (o: (typeof orders)[0]) => {
      const s = o['办件状态'] ?? '';
      return !s.includes('已办结');
    };
    const liveUnfinished = orders.filter(isUnfinished).length;
    // 数据回退链：merged_summary（合并口径）→ history_summary + 实时未办结 → 纯实时 → 加载中/失败
    let histStats: { total: number; unfinished: number; resolveRate: number } | null = null;
    let loadState: 'ok' | 'loading' | 'error' = 'ok';
    if (mergedSummary) {
      histStats = { total: mergedSummary.total, unfinished: mergedSummary.incomplete, resolveRate: mergedSummary.completion_rate };
    } else if (historyData) {
      const total = historyData.resolved + liveUnfinished;
      histStats = {
        total,
        unfinished: liveUnfinished,
        resolveRate: total ? Math.round((historyData.resolved / total) * 10000) / 100 : 0,
      };
    } else if (orders.length > 0) {
      histStats = {
        total: orders.length,
        unfinished: liveUnfinished,
        resolveRate: Math.round(((orders.length - liveUnfinished) / orders.length) * 10000) / 100,
      };
    } else if (loading || summaryStatus === 'loading') {
      loadState = 'loading';
    } else {
      loadState = 'error';
    }

    // 重办件统计：基于实时 orders 按事项小类分组取 TOP6（全部时间亦用实时数据计算）
    let bkd = data?.rework.breakdown ?? REWORK_BREAKDOWN;
    let rwTotal = data?.rework.reworkTotal ?? REWORK_ORDERS.reworkTotal;
    const base = timeRange === 'all' || !orders.length ? orders : (filterByTime(orders) as unknown as typeof orders);
    if (base.length) {
      const smallMap = new Map<string, number>();
      for (const o of base) {
        const s = stripCode(o['事项小类']) || '其他';
        smallMap.set(s, (smallMap.get(s) ?? 0) + 1);
      }
      const top6 = [...smallMap.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 6);
      rwTotal = top6.reduce((s, [, v]) => s + v, 0);
      bkd = top6.map(([type, count], i) => ({
        type, count,
        percent: base.length ? Math.round((count / base.length) * 1000) / 10 : 0,
        color: REWORK_COLORS[i % REWORK_COLORS.length],
      }));
    }

    return {
      stats: histStats,
      loadState,

```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# 从 public/data.json 真实工单按周统计生成 report_history，合并进 public/weekly_analysis.json
import json
from collections import defaultdict
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

ROOT = '/workspace/app-e3vpy5i0tji9'
AREAS = ['古田', '长丰大道', '城华路', '汉口', '丰美路', '长风路', '紫华路', '长天路']


def week_bounds(dt):
    """返回该日期所在周的周一、周日"""
    monday = dt - timedelta(days=dt.weekday())
    return monday, monday + timedelta(days=6)


def top_n(counter, n, name_key='name', count_key='count'):
    items = sorted(counter.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1], reverse=True)[:n]
    return [{name_key: k, count_key: v} for k, v in items]


def main():
    with open(f'{ROOT}/public/data.json', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        orders = json.load(f)

    # 按周分组（真实受理时间）
    weeks = defaultdict(list)
    for o in orders:
        t = str(o.get('受理时间') or '')
        try:
            dt = datetime.strptime(t[:10], '%Y-%m-%d')
        except ValueError:
            continue
        y, w, _ = dt.isocalendar()
        weeks[(y, w)].append((dt, o))

    # 取最近 12 个有数据的周，倒序（最新在前）
    keys = sorted(weeks.keys(), reverse=True)[:12]
    history = []
    for (y, w) in keys:
        items = weeks[(y, w)]
        monday, sunday = week_bounds(items[0][0])
        total = len(items)
        done = sum(1 for _, o in items if '已办结' in str(o.get('办件状态') or ''))
        undone = total - done

        issues = defaultdict(int)
        cats = defaultdict(int)
        areas = defaultdict(int)
        for _, o in items:
            sub = str(o.get('事项小类') or '').strip()
            cat = str(o.get('事项大类') or '').strip()
            if sub:
                issues[sub] += 1
            if cat:
                cats[cat] += 1
            text = f"{o.get('诉求主题') or ''}{o.get('诉求内容') or ''}"
            for a in AREAS:
                if a in text:
                    areas[a] += 1
                    break

        # key_cases：未办结优先 3 条真实案例
        cands = [o for _, o in items if '已办结' not in str(o.get('办件状态') or '')]
        cands = cands or [o for _, o in items]
        key_cases = [
            {
                'case_id': str(o.get('市级编号') or o.get('办件编号') or ''),
                'category': str(o.get('事项大类') or ''),
                'sub_category': str(o.get('事项小类') or ''),
                'content': str(o.get('诉求内容') or o.get('诉求主题') or '')[:120],
                'status': str(o.get('办件状态') or ''),
                'accept_time': str(o.get('受理时间') or ''),
            }
            for o in sorted(cands, key=lambda x: str(x.get('受理时间') or ''), reverse=True)[:3]
        ]

        top_issue = top_n(issues, 1)
        summary = (
            f"本周（{monday.strftime('%m月%d日')}-{sunday.strftime('%m月%d日')}）共受理诉求 {total} 件，"
            f"已办结 {done} 件、未办结 {undone} 件。"
            + (f"受理最多的事项为「{top_issue[0]['name']}」（{top_issue[0]['count']} 件）。" if top_issue else '')
        )

        history.append({
            'id': f'{y}-W{w:02d}',
            'week_of': monday.strftime('%Y-%m-%d'),
            'week_to': sunday.strftime('%Y-%m-%d'),
            'title': f'第{w}周诉求分析报告',
            'summary': summary,
            'total': total,
            'completed': done,
            'incomplete': undone,
            'top_issues': top_n(issues, 5),
            'top_categories': top_n(cats, 5),
            'hot_areas': top_n(areas, 5),
            'key_cases': key_cases,
        })

    # 合并进 weekly_analysis.json（保留现有全部键）
    with open(f'{ROOT}/public/weekly_analysis.json', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        data = json.load(f)
    data['report_history'] = history
    with open(f'{ROOT}/public/weekly_analysis.json', 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(',', ':'))

    print(f'生成 {len(history)} 份周报（倒序）:')
    for r in history[:5]:
        print(f"  {r['id']} {r['title']} {r['week_of']}~{r['week_to']} 共{r['total']}件")


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

import io, json, re
ROOT = '/workspace/app-e3vpy5i0tji9'

# ---- 1. index.css: kpi-num 字号缩小防溢出 ----
css_p = f'{ROOT}/src/index.css'

css = css.replace('  .kpi-num {\n    font-size: 26px;', '  .kpi-num {\n    font-size: 19px;')
io.open(css_p, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(css)
print('index.css kpi-num 26px->19px:', 'font-size: 19px' in css)

# ---- 2. dataService.ts: ReportHistoryItem 类型 + fallback 前5条真实周报 ----
hist = json.load(open(f'{ROOT}/public/weekly_analysis.json', encoding='utf-8'))['report_history'][:5]
def py2ts(obj, indent):
    sp = '  ' * indent
    if isinstance(obj, dict):
        lines = [f'{sp}{{']
        for k, v in obj.items():
            lines.append(f'{sp}  {k}: {py2ts(v, indent + 1)},')
        lines.append(f'{sp}}}')
        return '\n'.join(lines)
    if isinstance(obj, list):
        if not obj: return '[]'
        inner = ',\n'.join(py2ts(x, indent + 1) for x in obj)
        return f'[\n{inner},\n{sp}]'
    if isinstance(obj, bool): return 'true' if obj else 'false'
    if isinstance(obj, (int, float)): return str(obj)
    return json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False)

fallback_lit = py2ts(hist, 1)
type_block = f'''
// ============ 周报历史（weekly_analysis.json report_history，由 data.json 真实工单按周统计生成） ============

export interface WeeklyReportItem {{
  case_id: string;
  category: string;
  sub_category: string;
  content: string;
  status: string;
  accept_time: string;
}}

export interface ReportHistoryItem {{
  id: string;
  week_of: string;
  week_to: string;
  title: string;
  summary: string;
  total: number;
  completed: number;
  incomplete: number;
  top_issues: {{ name: string; count: number }}[];
  top_categories: {{ name: string; count: number }}[];
  hot_areas: {{ name: string; count: number }}[];
  key_cases: WeeklyReportItem[];
}}

/** 周报历史兜底基准（前 5 份真实周报，构建内联） */
export const REPORT_HISTORY_FALLBACK: ReportHistoryItem[] =
{fallback_lit};
'''

svc_p = f'{ROOT}/src/services/dataService.ts'
svc = io.open(svc_p, encoding='utf-8').read()

# WeeklyAnalysis 接口加 report_history 字段
svc = svc.replace(
  '  typical_cases: TypicalCase[];\n  key_supervision: KeySupervisionItem[];\n}',
  '  typical_cases: TypicalCase[];\n  key_supervision: KeySupervisionItem[];\n  /** 历史周报列表（由 data.json 真实工单按周统计生成，倒序最新在前） */\n  report_history?: ReportHistoryItem[];\n}'
)
# WEEKLY_ANALYSIS_FALLBACK 末尾加 report_history
svc = re.sub(
  r'(export const WEEKLY_ANALYSIS_FALLBACK: WeeklyAnalysis = \{[\s\S]*?\n\};)',
  lambda m: m.group(1).rstrip()[:-1].rstrip().rstrip(',') + f',\n  report_history: REPORT_HISTORY_FALLBACK,\n}};',
  svc, count=1
)
# 类型块插在 WEEKLY_ANALYSIS_FALLBACK 定义之前
svc = svc.replace('export const WEEKLY_ANALYSIS_FALLBACK: WeeklyAnalysis = {', type_block.strip() + '\n\nexport const WEEKLY_ANALYSIS_FALLBACK: WeeklyAnalysis = {', 1)
io.open(svc_p, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(svc)

// 每周分析报告模块：本周/上周总量、办结/未办结、环比变化、本周 TOP5 问题 + 历史报告弹窗
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { motion } from 'motion/react';
import { ArrowDown, ArrowUp, CalendarRange, ChevronLeft, Clock, FileBarChart, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';
import { useWeeklyAnalysis } from '@/contexts/WeeklyAnalysisContext';
import type { ReportHistoryItem } from '@/services/dataService';
import CountUp from './CountUp';
import PanelShell from './PanelShell';

/** 历史报告列表 + 详情弹窗（红金政务风） */
function ReportHistoryModal({ open, onClose }: { open: boolean; onClose: () => void }) {
  const { data } = useWeeklyAnalysis();
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<ReportHistoryItem | null>(null);
  const reports = data.report_history ?? [];
  if (!open) return null;

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center bg-background/70 backdrop-blur-sm"
      onClick={onClose}
    >
      <div
        className="screen-panel corner-decor animate-modal-in flex h-[80vh] max-h-[80vh] w-[900px] max-w-[90vw] flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-primary/40 shadow-[0_0_48px_rgba(212,168,67,0.25)]"
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2.5 px-5 pt-4 pb-2.5">
          <FileBarChart className="h-5 w-5 shrink-0 text-primary" />
          <h3 className="text-xl font-bold tracking-wide text-foreground">诉求分析报告</h3>
          <span className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">历史周报（{reports.length} 份）</span>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="ml-auto grid h-6 w-6 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded border border-border/70 bg-secondary/40 text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:border-risk-high/50 hover:text-risk-high"
            aria-label="关闭"
          >
            <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          </button>
        </div>
        <div className="title-bar mx-5 h-px shrink-0 opacity-70" />

        <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-hidden p-5">
          {selected ? (
            /* ---------- 报告详情 ---------- */
            <div className="flex h-full flex-col gap-3">
              <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setSelected(null)}
                  className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded border border-primary/50 px-2 py-1 text-xs text-primary transition-colors hover:bg-primary/10"
                >
                  <ChevronLeft className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />返回列表
                </button>
                <span className="truncate text-base font-bold text-foreground">{selected.title}</span>
                <span className="ml-auto shrink-0 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                  {selected.week_of} ~ {selected.week_to}
                </span>
              </div>

              <div className="shrink-0 rounded border border-border/30 bg-secondary/15 p-3 text-sm leading-relaxed text-foreground">
                {selected.summary}
              </div>

              <div className="grid shrink-0 grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                {[
                  { label: '受理件数', value: selected.total },
                  { label: '已办结', value: selected.completed },
                  { label: '未办结', value: selected.incomplete },
                ].map((k) => (
                  <div key={k.label} className="rounded border border-border/30 bg-background/40 px-3 py-2.5 text-center">
                    <p className="mb-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{k.label}</p>
                    <p className="num-glow font-digital text-2xl font-bold text-primary">{k.value}</p>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>

              <div className="hre-scroll min-h-0 flex-1 space-y-3 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
                <div>
                  <p className="mb-1.5 text-sm font-semibold text-foreground">高频问题 TOP5</p>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-1.5">
                    {selected.top_issues.map((it, i) => (
                      <div key={it.name} className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded border border-border/20 bg-secondary/10 px-2.5 py-1.5">
                        <span className="flex h-5 w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-primary/20 text-[11px] font-bold text-primary">{i + 1}</span>
                        <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-sm text-foreground">{it.name}</span>
                        <span className="shrink-0 font-digital text-sm font-bold text-primary">{it.count} 件</span>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>

                {selected.hot_areas.length > 0 && (
                  <div>
                    <p className="mb-1.5 text-sm font-semibold text-foreground">热点区域 TOP5</p>
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                      {selected.hot_areas.map((a) => (
                        <span key={a.name} className="rounded border border-primary/40 bg-primary/10 px-2 py-1 text-xs text-primary">
                          {a.name} · {a.count} 件
                        </span>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                {selected.key_cases.length > 0 && (
                  <div>
                    <p className="mb-1.5 text-sm font-semibold text-foreground">重点案件</p>
                    <div className="space-y-1.5">
                      {selected.key_cases.map((c) => (
                        <div key={c.case_id} className="rounded border border-border/25 bg-secondary/10 p-2.5">
                          <div className="mb-1 flex items-center gap-2">
                            <span className="font-digital text-xs text-primary">{c.case_id}</span>
                            <span className="ml-auto shrink-0 text-[10px] text-foreground">{c.sub_category || c.category}</span>
                            <span
                              className={cn(
                                'shrink-0 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold',
                                c.status.includes('已办结') ? 'bg-success/15 text-success' : 'bg-risk-high/15 text-risk-high',
                              )}
                            >
                              {c.status}
                            </span>
                          </div>
                          <p className="text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">{c.content}</p>
                        </div>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            /* ---------- 报告列表（倒序，最新在前） ---------- */
            <div className="hre-scroll h-full space-y-2 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
              {reports.map((r, i) => (
                <motion.button
                  type="button"
                  key={r.id}
                  initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
                  animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                  transition={{ delay: 0.03 * i }}
                  onClick={() => setSelected(r)}
                  className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded border border-border/25 bg-secondary/10 px-3.5 py-2.5 text-left transition-colors hover:border-primary/50 hover:bg-primary/10"
                >
                  <span className="grid h-9 w-9 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full border border-primary/40 bg-primary/15 text-primary">
                    <FileBarChart className="h-4 w-4" />
                  </span>
                  <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                    <span className="flex items-baseline gap-2">
                      <span className="truncate text-sm font-bold text-foreground">{r.title}</span>
                      <span className="shrink-0 text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">{r.week_of} ~ {r.week_to}</span>
                    </span>
                    <span className="mt-0.5 block truncate text-xs text-muted-foreground">{r.summary}</span>
                  </span>
                  <span className="flex shrink-0 items-baseline gap-1">
                    <span className="num-glow font-digital text-lg font-bold text-primary">{r.total}</span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">件</span>
                  </span>
                </motion.button>
              ))}
              {reports.length === 0 && (
                <p className="py-10 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">暂无历史报告数据</p>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function WeeklyReport({ className }: { className?: string }) {
  const { data } = useWeeklyAnalysis();
  const wr = data.weekly_report;
  const [modalOpen, setModalOpen] = useState(false);

  const kpis = useMemo(
    () => [
      { label: '本周总量', value: wr.this_week.total, change: wr.change.total },
      { label: '本周已办结', value: wr.this_week.completed, change: wr.change.completed },
      { label: '本周未办结', value: wr.this_week.incomplete },
    ],
    [wr],
  );

  return (
    <PanelShell
      title="每周分析报告"
      icon={<CalendarRange className="h-4 w-4" />}
      className={className}
      actions={
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setModalOpen(true)}
          className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded border border-primary/60 bg-primary/10 px-2 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium text-primary transition-colors hover:bg-primary/25"
        >
          <FileBarChart className="h-3 w-3" />
          分析报告
        </button>
      }
    >
      <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between rounded border border-border/30 bg-secondary/15 px-2.5 py-1.5">
        <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
          <CalendarRange className="h-3 w-3 text-primary" />
          {wr.week_of} ~ {wr.to}
        </span>
        <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">
          <Clock className="h-3 w-3" />
          上周 {wr.last_week.total} 件
        </span>
      </div>

      <div className="mb-2 grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
        {kpis.map((k) => (
          <div key={k.label} className="rounded border border-border/30 bg-background/40 px-1.5 py-2 text-center">
            <p className="mb-1 text-[10px] leading-tight text-muted-foreground">{k.label}</p>
            <p className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1 leading-none">
              <CountUp value={k.value} group className="num-glow font-digital text-lg font-bold text-primary" />
              {typeof k.change === 'number' && (
                <span
                  className={cn(
                    'flex items-center text-[10px] font-semibold',
                    k.change < 0 ? 'text-success' : k.change > 0 ? 'text-risk-high' : 'text-muted-foreground',
                  )}
                >
                  {k.change < 0 ? <ArrowDown className="h-3 w-3" /> : k.change > 0 ? <ArrowUp className="h-3 w-3" /> : null}
                  {Math.abs(k.change)}
                </span>
              )}
            </p>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="min-h-0 flex-1">
        <p className="mb-1.5 text-[11px] font-semibold text-foreground">本周 TOP5 问题</p>
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          {wr.top_issues_this_week.map((it, i) => (
            <motion.div
              key={it.name}
              initial={{ opacity: 0, x: 12 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
              transition={{ delay: 0.05 * i }}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded border border-border/20 bg-secondary/10 px-2 py-1.5"
            >
              <span className="flex h-5 w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-primary/20 text-[11px] font-bold text-primary">
                {i + 1}
              </span>
              <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-xs text-foreground">{it.name}</span>
              <span className="shrink-0 font-digital text-sm font-bold text-primary">{it.count}</span>
            </motion.div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      <ReportHistoryModal open={modalOpen} onClose={() => setModalOpen(false)} />
    </PanelShell>
  );
}
```

        smallMap.set(s, (smallMap.get(s) ?? 0) + 1);

        type, count,

        color: REWORK_COLORS[i % REWORK_COLORS.length],
      }));

      stats: histStats,
      loadState,
      trend: data?.kpiTrend ?? HOTLINE_STATS.kpiTrend,
      breakdown: bkd,
      reworkTotal: rwTotal,

===== IssuesRegionTabs Tab 写法参考 =====
// 左侧合并模块：高频问题排行 / 重点区域特征（内部 2 页签切换）
import { useState } from 'react';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';
import { BarChart3, MapPinned } from 'lucide-react';
import PanelShell from './PanelShell';
import TopIssuesChart from './TopIssuesChart';
import RegionFeatures from './RegionFeatures';

type SubTabKey = 'issues' | 'region';

const SUB_TABS: { key: SubTabKey; label: string; icon: typeof BarChart3 }[] = [
  { key: 'issues', label: '高频问题排行', icon: BarChart3 },
  { key: 'region', label: '重点区域特征', icon: MapPinned },
];

interface IssuesRegionTabsProps {
  className?: string;
}

export default function IssuesRegionTabs({ className }: IssuesRegionTabsProps) {
  const [active, setActive] = useState<SubTabKey>('issues');

  return (
    <PanelShell className={className} title="问题分析" subtitle="排行 / 区域特征" icon={<BarChart3 className="h-4 w-4" />}>
      <div className="flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col gap-2">
        {/* 页签栏 */}
        <div className="flex shrink-0 gap-4 border-b border-border/60">
          {SUB_TABS.map((tab) => {
            const Icon = tab.icon;
            const isActive = active === tab.key;
            return (
              <button
                key={tab.key}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setActive(tab.key)}
                className={cn(
                  'flex items-center gap-1.5 border-b-2 pb-1.5 text-sm font-medium transition-colors',
                  isActive ? 'border-primary text-primary' : 'border-transparent text-muted-foreground hover:text-primary/80'
                )}
              >
                <Icon className={cn('h-3.5 w-3.5', isActive ? 'text-primary' : 'text-muted-foreground')} />
                {tab.label}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {/* 内容：平滑过渡 */}
        <div key={active} className="animate-tab-in min-h-0 flex-1">
          {active === 'issues' ? (
            <TopIssuesChart className="h-full" bare />
          ) : (
            <RegionFeatures className="h-full" bare />
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </PanelShell>
  );
}

// 左侧饼图合并模块：高频问题 / 事项大类（内部 Tab 切换）
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Layers, PieChart } from 'lucide-react';
import type { EChartsCoreOption } from 'echarts';
import { useECharts } from '@/lib/echarts';
import { useWeeklyAnalysis } from '@/contexts/WeeklyAnalysisContext';
import PanelShell from './PanelShell';

const CAT_COLORS = ['#c41e24', '#d4a843', '#e63c40', '#b8963f', '#a01820', '#f0c860', '#8b1014', '#e8b94a'];

type TabKey = 'highfreq' | 'category';

export default function LeftPieTabs({ className }: { className?: string }) {
  const { data } = useWeeklyAnalysis();
  const [active, setActive] = useState<TabKey>('highfreq');

  const highFreq = data.high_freq_issues_pie;
  const category = data.category_pie;
  const cityMgmt = data.city_mgmt_pie;
  const mgmtTotal = cityMgmt.reduce((s, c) => s + c.value, 0);
  const mgmtRatio = mgmtTotal ? Math.round((cityMgmt[0]?.value / mgmtTotal) * 10000) / 100 : 0;

  const option = useMemo<EChartsCoreOption>(() => {
    const baseTooltip = {
      trigger: 'item' as const,
      backgroundColor: 'rgba(26,15,10,0.92)',
      borderColor: 'rgba(212,168,67,0.3)',
      textStyle: { color: '#fff5e6', fontSize: 13 },
      formatter: (p: { name: string; value: number; percent: number }) =>
        `${p.name}<br/>${p.value} 件 · ${p.percent}%`,
    };
    const labelStyle = {
      formatter: '{b}\n{c} 件 · {d}%',
      fontSize: 11,
      color: 'rgba(255,245,230,0.85)',
      lineHeight: 14,
    };

    if (active === 'highfreq') {
      return {
        tooltip: baseTooltip,
        series: [
          {
            type: 'pie',
            center: ['50%', '52%'],
            radius: ['40%', '66%'],
            avoidLabelOverlap: true,
            itemStyle: { borderColor: 'rgba(12,8,6,0.85)', borderWidth: 2 },
            label: { show: true, ...labelStyle },
            labelLine: { length: 8, length2: 8, smooth: true },
            emphasis: {
              label: { show: true, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 'bold', color: '#fff5e6' },
              itemStyle: { shadowBlur: 16, shadowColor: 'rgba(212,168,67,0.5)' },
            },
            data: highFreq.map((s, i) => ({
              name: s.name,
              value: s.value,
              itemStyle: { color: CAT_COLORS[i % CAT_COLORS.length] },
            })),
          },
        ],
      };
    }
    // 事项大类 + 大城管占比双环
    return {
      tooltip: baseTooltip,
      series: [
        {
          type: 'pie',
          center: ['50%', '52%'],
          radius: ['44%', '66%'],
          itemStyle: { borderColor: 'rgba(12,8,6,0.85)', borderWidth: 2 },
          label: { show: true, ...labelStyle },
          labelLine: { length: 8, length2: 8, smooth: true },
          emphasis: {
            label: { show: true, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 'bold', color: '#fff5e6' },
            itemStyle: { shadowBlur: 16, shadowColor: 'rgba(212,168,67,0.5)' },
          },
          data: category.map((s, i) => ({
            name: s.name,
            value: s.value,
            itemStyle: { color: CAT_COLORS[i % CAT_COLORS.length] },
          })),
        },
      ],
    };
  }, [active, highFreq, category]);

  const { containerRef } = useECharts(option);

  return (
    <PanelShell
      title="问题结构分析"
      icon={<PieChart className="h-4 w-4" />}
      className={className}
    >
      <div className="flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col gap-2">
        {/* Tab 切换栏 */}
        <div className="flex shrink-0 gap-4 border-b border-border/60">
          {[
            { key: 'highfreq' as TabKey, label: '高频问题', icon: PieChart },
            { key: 'category' as TabKey, label: '事项大类', icon: Layers },
          ].map((tab) => {
            const Icon = tab.icon;
            const isActive = active === tab.key;
            return (
              <button
                key={tab.key}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setActive(tab.key)}
                className="flex items-center gap-1.5 border-b-2 pb-1.5 text-sm font-medium transition-colors"
                style={{
                  borderColor: isActive ? '#d4a843' : 'transparent',
                  color: isActive ? '#d4a843' : 'rgba(255,245,230,0.55)',
                }}
              >
                <Icon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" style={{ color: isActive ? '#d4a843' : 'rgba(255,245,230,0.55)' }} />
                {tab.label}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        <div ref={containerRef} className="min-h-0 flex-1" />
        {active === 'category' && (
          <p className="shrink-0 text-center text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">
            大城管（城市市容管理类）占比 <span className="font-digital font-bold text-primary">{mgmtRatio}%</span>
          </p>
        )}
      </div>
    </PanelShell>
  );
}

        series: [
          {
            type: 'pie',
            center: ['50%', '52%'],
            radius: ['42%', '66%'],
            itemStyle: { borderColor: 'rgba(12,8,6,0.85)', borderWidth: 2 },
            label: { show: true, ...labelStyle },
            labelLine: { length: 8, length2: 8, smooth: true },
            emphasis: {
              label: { show: true, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 'bold', color: '#fff5e6' },
              itemStyle: { shadowBlur: 16, shadowColor: 'rgba(212,168,67,0.5)' },
            },
            data: cityMgmt.map((s, i) => ({
              name: s.name,
              value: s.value,
              itemStyle: { color: i === 0 ? '#c41e24' : '#d4a843' },
            })),
          },
        ],
      };
    }
    return {
      tooltip: baseTooltip,
      series: [
        {
          type: 'pie',
          center: ['50%', '52%'],
          radius: ['40%', '66%'],
          avoidLabelOverlap: true,
          itemStyle: { borderColor: 'rgba(12,8,6,0.85)', borderWidth: 2 },
          label: { show: true, ...labelStyle },
          labelLine: { length: 8, length2: 8, smooth: true },
          emphasis: {
            label: { show: true, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 'bold', color: '#fff5e6' },
            itemStyle: { shadowBlur: 16, shadowColor: 'rgba(212,168,67,0.5)' },
          },
          data: dept.map((s, i) => ({
            name: s.name,
            value: s.value,
            itemStyle: { color: COLORS[i % COLORS.length] },
          })),
        },
      ],
    };
  }, [active, cityMgmt, dept]);

  const { containerRef } = useECharts(option);

  return (
    <PanelShell
      title="构成分析"
      icon={<ShieldCheck className="h-4 w-4" />}
      className={className}
    >
      <div className="flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col gap-2">
        {/* Tab 切换栏 */}
        <div className="flex shrink-0 gap-4 border-b border-border/60">
          {[
            { key: 'citymgmt' as TabKey, label: '大城管占比', icon: ShieldCheck },
            { key: 'dept' as TabKey, label: '科室划分', icon: Building2 },
          ].map((tab) => {
            const Icon = tab.icon;
            const isActive = active === tab.key;
            return (
              <button
                key={tab.key}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setActive(tab.key)}
                className="flex items-center gap-1.5 border-b-2 pb-1.5 text-sm font-medium transition-colors"
                style={{
                  borderColor: isActive ? '#d4a843' : 'transparent',
                  color: isActive ? '#d4a843' : 'rgba(255,245,230,0.55)',
                }}
              >
                <Icon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" style={{ color: isActive ? '#d4a843' : 'rgba(255,245,230,0.55)' }} />
                {tab.label}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        <div ref={containerRef} className="min-h-0 flex-1" />
      </div>
    </PanelShell>
  );
}
```
